Third-party notices for the Velopack native binaries (vpk 1.2.161: setup.exe / setup_x64.exe, update.exe /
update_x64.exe, stub.exe / stub_x64.exe), which vpk embeds in InterviewAssistant-Setup.exe and installs as
Update.exe and the launcher stub next to the application.
==============================================================================
These executables are compiled from Velopack's MIT-licensed Rust code (see Velopack.txt) and statically link
Rust crates. The vpk package ships no notices for them (its vendor/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md covers only build-time
tools: signtool, zstd.exe, AppImage runtime, mksquashfs, WiX).

How this list was produced (236 crates):
  * 83 crates CONFIRMED: names and exact versions read from the crates.io source paths compiled into the
    six Windows binaries of vpk 1.2.161, plus zstd-sys 2.1.0+zstd.1.5.7 (the bundled Zstandard C library used
    by zstd-safe).
  * 153 crates marked '*' in the index: reachable from velopack_bins / velopack / velopack_l18n in Cargo.lock at
    commit 92d6a1c91716729d449034df5c50307dcce39493 when only normal (non-build, non-dev) dependencies active
    on x86_64/i686-pc-windows-msvc are followed and proc-macro crates are excluded. They leave no source path in
    the binaries, so this part is a superset: some of them may not actually be linked.
Each crate's declared license comes from its Cargo.toml and the texts are copied verbatim from the license files
inside the published crate archive (static.crates.io). Where a crate offers a choice of licenses that includes
MIT, MIT is elected and only that text is reproduced; GPL alternatives are never elected (zstd: BSD-3-Clause,
self_cell: Apache-2.0).
The Rust standard library (MIT OR Apache-2.0, The Rust Project Developers) is also statically linked; MIT is
elected - the Rust project's MIT text is reproduced in the normpath entry ([LICENSE-THIRD-PARTY]).
VERIFY: for a definitive list, run cargo-about (or cargo-license) on the velopack repository at the commit above
for the x86_64-pc-windows-msvc / i686-pc-windows-msvc targets with the features vpk builds with.
